receive & Grace for Grace Tis the[^028-note67] will of Christ to convey this most precious Golden oil this most excellent & precious Benefit that ever he bestows by ministers whereby as X hims is the Author of Et. salva. so ministers become a Kind of subordinate saviours Thus the apostle speaks of his saving his Hearers Rom. 11. 14. 1 Cor. 9. 22. & tells in Tim. that in following his Counsel He will save ----- 1 Tim. 4. 16. -- & tis in in the Prophecy Obad. v. 21. ministers are called saviours If [ ] we have[^028-note68] this In this way we [-] shall not only have that anointing that will qualify us for a most honourable office in this O but we shall be anointed to be th an Eternal Priesthood. & Not only so but an Eternal Royalty in Heaven & shall be there Kings & Priests to G. even the Father [-] In this way we shall stand by the God of the Earth in this O & we shall be in Enjoymt much holy & sweet Communion with Him & th in this we shall Come to be near his Throne in Heaven[^028-note69] faithful & Holy ministers will be some probably some that will be next to X in Heaven in Johns vision of the Glory of G. in Heaven Rev. 5. the Elders 24 Elders were represented as nex having their seats next the Throne ____________And this will be the way to sucess in our work . it will be the way indeed to communicate that Golden oil of the saving Grace of G. to the Hearts of our Hearers [-] T Tis manifested to be the will of G. that by this means his Chh. should be supported &[^028-note70] upheld in the O & that by this means it should prevail & be gloriously advanced G. when it was a very dark time with the Jewish Chh. G. gave the Proph. Zech. this vision that we have an account of in the text & Context to Encourage his People that his Chh. should prevail & finally be Glorious advanced by & he was shewn by what means it was to be viz. by his f a holy & faithful ministry.

_____

Thus we shall be stars in Xs right Hand full of spiritual Light our selves & the meaning of v[^028-note71] & shall shine in Gods Chh. & be means of Enlightening that & making Gods si People to shine also & hereafter star after we have thus shone as stars in X Right Hand on Earth shall be exalted hereafter to shine as the brightness of the Firmam the stars forever & ever in a O of Glory agreable to that blessed Promise dan. 12. 3. and They that be wise shall shine as the brightness of the Firmament

______ ____________

[^028-note72]

_____________________________________

2. I would apply my self to the People of this Chh & Congregation whom G. after long continued his f Great & Long continued Frowns is this day smiling on . giving Them another Pastor one whom has been already for a Considerable Time proved in the work of the ministry & whom there is so great Reason to hope is a son of oil my Exhortation to you is that you approve[^028-note73] your selves a Golden Candlestick a holy society & truly Xtian Church as as such treat & Let your Behaviour toward your Pastor be becoming such a society receiving Him & Ever Treating him as one [ ] as an anointed one one anointed of G. to the h same d office among you X the God of the of X the Immanuel and G. of the Earth to this sacred office among you being Be as the Golden Candlestick in the text contantly adhering to this O Olive Branch & constantly imbibing from Him the Golden oil through the Golden Pipes of Gods[^028-note74] ordinances that by this means your Lamps may burn & your Light so shine before men that They seeing your Good works may glori fy your F. which is in H.

______

& Let me repeat this to you do it & inculcate it upon you that you do this in the by the seek this Golden oil. throu in the way of Gods ordinances which are meant by the Golden Pipes in the Text . & by Gods[^028-note75] ministers regularly introduced. & not by Lay Teachers & exhorters in the way of Gods Institution . [-] being not only [-] inwardly moved but externally & regularly set introduced into their office as Joshua & [-] Zerubbabel were for such offices it is most apparent there are meant by the one in the Text by the anointed ones that stand by the God of the whole Earth. seek the Golden oil in this way & not by Lay Teachers & Exhorters rejecting a regular ministry. this will They that do so whatever They may think of thems. & lift thems. up in their own conceit as if they were[^028-note76] the only pure Chh. & may Call others antiXtians yet they surely are not of the Golden Candlestick that is spoken of in the Text nor are in They in the way of imbi to receive the true Golden oil & sin & sincere milk of the word but in the way to Corruption & Confusion They take care in the w in the way to imbibe the delusions of the devil & his sp. instead to receive his sp. instead of the Golden oil

______

& dont hearken to them that call Gods ordinances by the name of dead Forms which the Text Represent as Golden Pipes such men are guilty of the most[^028-note77] horrid profaneness & Presumption that talk thus of the Holy ordinan ces of Gods appointmt & pretend that the Time is Come when G. will have done with mi the office ordinances & the office of the ministry & is about to introduce a new dispensation I scruple not to say that such men that industri ously endeavour to propagate such opinions as those are the devils Emissaries own Emmisaries & the[^028-note78] devils Instruments when G. introduces the glor. Times of the Chh according to his Promise he will not be by Lay Teachers as some pretend put down ministers & exalt Lay Teachers in their Room as some pretend The Text & context Represents the matter otherwise -- Chh Chh will be advanced in that way -- [-] there [-] seems in the text a special Reference to that future Glo. day of the Chhs in calling & the [ ] Redemption from the[^028-note79] sp. Babilon of which that Restoration of the Jewish Chh. by Joshua & Zerubbabel after the Captivity in the literal Babilon was a Type [-] and theref X in the Text is called the God of the whole Earth with Reference to the Time when all the Ends of the Earth should look to him & be saved

______

Theref. take heed that you dont hearken to those Emmissaries that [] wander are sent out & wander to & fro to propagate the forementioned corrupt notions There never has been any[^028-note80] Good Effect of such like Emissaries & notions The O has formerly abounded with just such Persons Persons -- or the very same notions & practices ----- particularly abounded a little after the Reformation

_________

They are poor deluded Creatures deceiving & being deceived wandring stars & I wish I could not say that I fear they are reserved for the Blackness of darkness forever follow not their vain[^028-note81] notions but Cleave to Gods ordinances & imbibe the Golden oil by those Golden Pipes from Gods [ ] those officers that are Gods anointed ones it will be the way the way to an Evi dence of your being

indeed a Golden Candlestick . & to be which having your Lamps fed with fewel oil. shines bright with a heaven ly Light maintained by fire from Heaven & not instead of that to be enkindled have the Light that is in you to be darkness & to be[^028-note82] by enflamed with a false Zeal inkindled by the fire of Hell the bottomless Pit. ______

doc. In saving Conversion sinners are turned from the Power of satan unto God 1. sinners while unconverted are under the Power of satan 2. when savingly converted they are turned from his Power. 3. In saving Conversion they are turned to G.
